While global geospatial foundation models offer powerful, generalizable representations, directly fusing their high-dimensional implicit embeddings with high-resolution visual features frequently triggers feature interference and spatial structure degradation due to a severe semantic-spatial gap. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Structure-Semantic Decoupled Modulation (SSDM) framework, which decouples global geospatial representations into two complementary cross-modal injection pathways. First, the structural prior modulation branch introduces the macroscopic receptive field priors from global representations into the self-attention modules of the high-resolution encoder. By guiding local feature extraction with holistic structural constraints, it effectively suppresses prediction fragmentation caused by high-frequency detail noise and excessive intra-class variance. Second, the global semantic injection branch explicitly aligns holistic context with the deep high-resolution feature space and directly supplements global semantics via cross-modal integration, thereby significantly enhancing the semantic consistency and category-level discrimination of complex land covers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing cross-modal fusion approaches. By unleashing the potential of global embeddings, SSDM consistently improves high-resolution mapping accuracy across diverse scenarios, providing a universal and effective paradigm for integrating geospatial foundation models into high-resolution vision tasks.

</details>


### [76] [PC2Model: ISPRS benchmark on 3D point cloud to model registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19596)
*Mehdi Maboudi,Said Harb,Jackson Ferrao,Kourosh Khoshelham,Yelda Turkan,Karam Mawas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PC2Model基准数据集：结合模拟与真实点云的点云到模型配准基准，支持经典与数据驱动方法的训练评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云配准在建筑监测、自动驾驶、机器人、VR/AR等应用中至关重要。随着LiDAR等获取技术普及和深度学习发展，研究重点转向下游任务，特别是点云到模型(PC2Model)配准。数据驱动方法面临真实扫描中的稀疏性、噪声、杂乱和遮挡问题，性能受限，需要专门基准来支持方法训练和评估。

Method: 提出PC2Model基准数据集，采用混合设计：结合模拟点云（部分情况下包含真实扫描）及其对应的3D模型。模拟数据提供精确真值和受控条件，真实数据引入传感器和环境伪影。该设计支持跨领域鲁棒训练和评估，并能够系统分析模型从模拟到真实场景的可迁移性。

Result: 开发了公开可用的PC2Model基准数据集，由ICWG II/Ib领导开发，数据集可通过https://zenodo.org/uploads/17581812公开访问。该基准支持经典方法和数据驱动方法的训练与评估。

Conclusion: PC2Model基准通过混合模拟与真实数据的设计，解决了点云到模型配准中数据驱动方法面临的现实挑战，为系统评估和模型可迁移性分析提供了标准化平台，推动了该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Point cloud registration involves aligning one point cloud with another or with a three-dimensional (3D) model, enabling the integration of multimodal data into a unified representation. This is essential in applications such as construction monitoring, autonomous driving, robotics, and virtual or augmented reality (VR/AR).With the increasing accessibility of point cloud acquisition technologies, such as Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) and structured light scanning, along with recent advances in deep learning, the research focus has increasingly shifted towards downstream tasks, particularly point cloud-to-model (PC2Model) registration. While data-driven methods aim to automate this process, they struggle with sparsity, noise, clutter, and occlusions in real-world scans, which limit their performance. To address these challenges, this paper introduces the PC2Model benchmark, a publicly available dataset designed to support the training and evaluation of both classical and data-driven methods. Developed under the leadership of ICWG II/Ib, the PC2Model benchmark adopts a hybrid design that combines simulated point clouds with, in some cases, real-world scans and their corresponding 3D models. Simulated data provide precise ground truth and controlled conditions, while real-world data introduce sensor and environmental artefacts. This design supports robust training and evaluation across domains and enables the systematic analysis of model transferability from simulated to real-world scenarios. The dataset is publicly accessible at: https://zenodo.org/uploads/17581812.

</details>


### [77] [GRAFT: Geometric Refinement and Fitting Transformer for Human Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19624)
*Pradyumna YM,Yuxuan Xue,Yue Chen,Nikita Kister,István Sárándi,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAFT是一个基于几何的快速3D人-场景交互重建方法，通过预测交互梯度迭代优化人体网格，在保持优化方法精度的同时实现50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D人-场景交互重建方法存在权衡：基于优化的方法准确但慢（~20秒），前馈方法快但缺乏显式交互推理，导致漂浮和穿透伪影。需要结合两者优势的方法。

Method: GRAFT通过学习的人-场景交互先验预测交互梯度（纠正参数更新），迭代优化人体网格。使用身体锚定令牌编码交互状态，通过几何探针捕捉与附近表面的空间关系。轻量级transformer循环更新网格并重新探测场景。

Result: GRAFT相比最先进的前馈方法提升交互质量达113%，匹配基于优化的交互质量但运行时间降低约50倍。在64.8%的三方用户研究中被偏好，能无缝泛化到野外多人物场景。

Conclusion: GRAFT成功将基于几何的人-场景拟合摊销为快速前馈推理，实现了高质量、快速的3D人-场景交互重建，可作为可转移的即插即用先验提升现有方法。

Abstract: Reconstructing physically plausible 3D human-scene interactions (HSI) from a single image currently presents a trade-off: optimization based methods offer accurate contact but are slow (~20s), while feed-forward approaches are fast yet lack explicit interaction reasoning, producing floating and interpenetration artifacts.
  Our key insight is that geometry-based human--scene fitting can be amortized into fast feed-forward inference. We present GRAFT (Geometric Refinement And Fitting Transformer), a learned HSI prior that predicts Interaction Gradients: corrective parameter updates that iteratively refine human meshes by reasoning about their 3D relationship to the surrounding scene.
  GRAFT encodes the interaction state into compact body-anchored tokens, each grounded in the scene geometry via Geometric Probes that capture spatial relationships with nearby surfaces.
  A lightweight transformer recurrently updates human meshes and re-probes the scene, ensuring the final pose aligns with both learned priors and observed geometry.
  GRAFT operates either as an end-to-end reconstructor using image features, or with geometry alone as a transferable plug-and-play HSI prior that improves feed-forward methods without retraining.
  Experiments show GRAFT improves interaction quality by up to 113% over state-of-the-art feed-forward methods and matches optimization-based interaction quality at ${\sim}50{\times}$ lower runtime, while generalizing seamlessly to in-the-wild multi-person scenes and being preferred in 64.8% of three-way user study. Project page: https://pradyumnaym.github.io/graft .

</details>


### [78] [CreatiParser: Generative Image Parsing of Raster Graphic Designs into Editable Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19632)
*Weidong Chen,Dexiang Hong,Zhendong Mao,Yutao Cheng,Xinyan Liu,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种混合生成框架，用于将栅格化图形设计图像解析为可编辑的图层结构（文本、背景、贴纸），通过视觉语言模型处理文本区域，多分支扩散架构处理背景和贴纸，并引入ParserReward和Group Relative Policy Optimization来对齐人类设计偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型通常输出无明确图层结构的栅格化图像，限制了后续编辑。现有的图形设计解析方法多采用多阶段流水线（布局预测、抠图、修复），存在误差累积和可控性有限的问题。

Method: 提出混合生成框架：1) 使用视觉语言模型将文本区域解析为文本渲染协议；2) 采用支持RGBA的多分支扩散架构生成背景和贴纸图层；3) 引入ParserReward并结合Group Relative Policy Optimization来优化生成质量。

Result: 在Parser-40K和Crello两个挑战性数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有方法，在所有指标上平均提升23.7%。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了从栅格化图像到可编辑图层的解析，解决了现有方法的误差累积和可控性限制问题，为图形设计图像提供了高质量的图层分解和编辑能力。

Abstract: Graphic design images consist of multiple editable layers, such as text, background, and decorative elements, while most generative models produce rasterized outputs without explicit layer structures, limiting downstream editing. Existing graphic design parsing methods typically rely on multi-stage pipelines combining layout prediction, matting, and inpainting, which suffer from error accumulation and limited controllability. We propose a hybrid generative framework for raster-to-layer graphic design parsing that decomposes a design image into editable text, background, and sticker layers. Text regions are parsed using a vision-language model into a text rendering protocol, enabling faithful reconstruction and flexible re-editing, while background and sticker layers are generated using a multi-branch diffusion architecture with RGBA support. We further introduce ParserReward and integrate it with Group Relative Policy Optimization to align generation quality with human design preferences. Extensive experiments on two challenging datasets, \emph{i.e.,} the Parser-40K and Crello datasets, demonstrate superior performance over existing methods, \emph{eg.,} achieving an overall average improvement of 23.7\% across all metrics.

</details>


### [79] [CoInteract: Physically-Consistent Human-Object Interaction Video Synthesis via Spatially-Structured Co-Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19636)
*Xiangyang Luo,Xiaozhe Xin,Tao Feng,Xu Guo,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoInteract是一个用于合成人-物交互视频的端到端框架，通过人类感知的专家混合和空间结构化协同生成技术，解决了现有扩散模型在结构稳定性和物理合理性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型在合成人-物交互视频时存在两个主要问题：1）手部和面部等敏感区域的结构稳定性不足；2）物理接触的合理性不足（如避免手-物体穿透）。这些问题限制了在电子商务、数字广告和虚拟营销等领域的实际应用价值。

Method: CoInteract基于扩散变换器架构，引入两个互补设计：1）人类感知的专家混合，通过空间监督路由将令牌分配到轻量级的区域专业化专家，以最小参数开销提高细粒度结构保真度；2）空间结构化协同生成，采用双流训练范式联合建模RGB外观流和辅助的HOI结构流，注入交互几何先验。训练时HOI流关注RGB令牌并监督共享骨干权重，推理时移除HOI分支实现零开销RGB生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，CoInteract在结构稳定性、逻辑一致性和交互真实性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoInteract通过创新的架构设计有效解决了人-物交互视频合成中的结构稳定性和物理合理性问题，为实际应用提供了高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Synthesizing human--object interaction (HOI) videos has broad practical value in e-commerce, digital advertising, and virtual marketing. However, current diffusion models, despite their photorealistic rendering capability, still frequently fail on (i) the structural stability of sensitive regions such as hands and faces and (ii) physically plausible contact (e.g., avoiding hand--object interpenetration). We present CoInteract, an end-to-end framework for HOI video synthesis conditioned on a person reference image, a product reference image, text prompts, and speech audio. CoInteract introduces two complementary designs embedded into a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) backbone. First, we propose a Human-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) that routes tokens to lightweight, region-specialized experts via spatially supervised routing, improving fine-grained structural fidelity with minimal parameter overhead. Second, we propose Spatially-Structured Co-Generation, a dual-stream training paradigm that jointly models an RGB appearance stream and an auxiliary HOI structure stream to inject interaction geometry priors. During training, the HOI stream attends to RGB tokens and its supervision regularizes shared backbone weights; at inference, the HOI branch is removed for zero-overhead RGB generation. Experimental results demonstrate that CoInteract significantly outperforms existing methods in structural stability, logical consistency, and interaction realism.

</details>


### [80] [MedFlowSeg: Flow Matching for Medical Image Segmentation with Frequency-Aware Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19675)
*Zhi Chen,Runze Hu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedFlowSeg：基于条件流匹配的医学图像分割框架，通过时间依赖向量场将先验分布传输到目标分割分布，实现一步确定性推理，相比扩散模型显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学图像分割的生成式方法主要基于扩散模型，存在计算开销大（需要迭代采样）和架构受限（通常基于UNet）的问题。流匹配作为一种原理性框架，能够学习连续时间传输映射，为医学图像分割提供更高效的理论基础。

Method: 提出MedFlowSeg条件流匹配框架，将医学图像分割建模为学习时间依赖向量场，将简单先验分布传输到目标分割分布。开发双重条件机制：1）双分支空间注意力模块注入多尺度结构信息；2）频率感知注意力模块通过差异感知融合和时间依赖调制建模空间与谱表示间的跨域交互。

Result: 在多种医学成像模态上进行广泛实验验证，MedFlowSeg达到最先进性能，同时相比基于扩散的方法显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 流匹配作为理论严谨且计算高效的替代方案，在生成式医学图像分割中具有巨大潜力。MedFlowSeg框架通过一步确定性推理保持生成式建模的表达能力，同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently emerged as a principled framework for learning continuous-time transport maps, enabling efficient deterministic generation without relying on stochastic diffusion processes. While generative modeling has shown promise for medical image segmentation, particularly in capturing uncertainty and complex anatomical variability, existing approaches are predominantly built upon diffusion models, which incur substantial computational overhead due to iterative sampling and are often constrained by UNet-based parameterizations. In this work, we introduce MedFlowSeg, a conditional flow matching framework that formulates medical image segmentation as learning a time-dependent vector field that transports a simple prior distribution to the target segmentation distribution. This formulation enables one-step deterministic inference while preserving the expressiveness of generative modeling. We further develop a dual-conditioning mechanism to incorporate structured priors into the learned flow. Specifically, we propose a Dual-Branch Spatial Attention module that injects multi-scale structural information into the flow field, and a Frequency-Aware Attention module that models cross-domain interactions between spatial and spectral representations via discrepancy-aware fusion and time-dependent modulation. Together, these components provide an effective parameterization of conditional flows that capture both global anatomical structure and fine-grained boundary details. We provide extensive empirical validation across multiple medical imaging modalities, demonstrating that MedFlowSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing computational cost compared to diffusion-based methods. Our results highlight the potential of flow matching as a theoretically grounded and computationally efficient alternative for generative medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [81] [MMControl: Unified Multi-Modal Control for Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19679)
*Liyang Li,Wen Wang,Canyu Zhao,Tianjian Feng,Zhiyue Zhao,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMControl：一种用于联合音视频生成的多模态控制框架，支持视觉和声学控制信号的组合控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控生成框架通常仅限于视频控制，限制了全面可控性并导致跨模态对齐不理想。需要支持多模态控制以实现更全面的音视频生成控制。

Method: 提出双流条件注入机制，将视觉和声学控制信号（参考图像、参考音频、深度图、姿态序列）通过旁路分支注入联合音视频扩散变换器。引入模态特定引导缩放，允许在推理时独立动态调整每个条件的影响强度。

Result: 实验表明，MMControl在联合音视频生成中实现了对角色身份、声音音色、身体姿态和场景布局的细粒度、可组合控制。

Conclusion: MMControl填补了现有可控生成框架的空白，通过多模态控制实现了更全面、更精确的联合音视频生成控制。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have enabled high-quality joint audio-video generation, producing videos with synchronized audio within a single model. However, existing controllable generation frameworks are typically restricted to video-only control. This restricts comprehensive controllability and often leads to suboptimal cross-modal alignment. To bridge this gap, we present MMControl, which enables users to perform Multi-Modal Control in joint audio-video generation. MMControl introduces a dual-stream conditional injection mechanism. It incorporates both visual and acoustic control signals, including reference images, reference audio, depth maps, and pose sequences, into a joint generation process. These conditions are injected through bypass branches into a joint audio-video Diffusion Transformer, enabling the model to simultaneously generate identity-consistent video and timbre-consistent audio under structural constraints. Furthermore, we introduce modality-specific guidance scaling, which allows users to independently and dynamically adjust the influence strength of each visual and acoustic condition at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MMControl achieves fine-grained, composable control over character identity, voice timbre, body pose, and scene layout in joint audio-video generation.

</details>


### [82] [IR-Flow: Bridging Discriminative and Generative Image Restoration via Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19680)
*Zihao Fan,Xin Lu,Jie Xiao,Dong Li,Jie Huang,Xueyang Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IR-Flow：基于Rectified Flow的统一图像修复框架，通过多级数据分布流和累积速度场，在判别式与生成式方法间架起桥梁，实现快速推理并保持失真-感知平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像修复方法存在局限性：单步判别式映射通过期望学习缺乏精细细节，而生成式方法则面临低效的多步采样和噪声-残差耦合问题。需要一种能弥合这两种范式差距的统一框架。

Method: 提出IR-Flow方法：1) 构建多级数据分布流，增强模型学习和适应不同退化水平的能力；2) 提出累积速度场，学习跨不同退化水平的传输轨迹，引导中间状态向干净目标；3) 引入多步一致性约束，确保轨迹连贯性并提升少步修复性能；4) 直接在退化与干净图像域间建立线性传输流。

Result: 在去雨、去噪和雨滴去除任务上的广泛评估表明，IR-Flow仅需少量采样步骤即可获得有竞争力的定量结果，提供了高效灵活且保持优异失真-感知平衡的框架。

Conclusion: IR-Flow成功弥合了判别式与生成式图像修复方法之间的差距，通过线性传输流实现了快速推理并增强了对分布外退化的适应性，为图像修复提供了统一高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In image restoration, single-step discriminative mappings often lack fine details via expectation learning, whereas generative paradigms suffer from inefficient multi-step sampling and noise-residual coupling. To address this dilemma, we propose IR-Flow, a novel image restoration method based on Rectified Flow that serves as a unified framework bridging the gap between discriminative and generative paradigms. Specifically, we first construct multilevel data distribution flows, which expand the ability of models to learn from and adapt to various levels of degradation. Subsequently, cumulative velocity fields are proposed to learn transport trajectories across varying degradation levels, guiding intermediate states toward the clean target, while a multi-step consistency constraint is presented to enforce trajectory coherence and boost few-step restoration performance. We show that directly establishing a linear transport flow between degraded and clean image domains not only enables fast inference but also improves adaptability to out-of-distribution degradations. Extensive evaluations on deraining, denoising and raindrop removal tasks demonstrate that IR-Flow achieves competitive quantitative results with only a few sampling steps, offering an efficient and flexible framework that maintains an excellent distortion-perception balance. Our code is available at https://github.com/fanzh03/IR-Flow.

</details>


### [83] [Unveiling Fine-Grained Visual Traces: Evaluating Multimodal Interleaved Reasoning Chains in Multimodal STEM Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19697)
*Jing Jin,Hao Liu,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou,Zhenke Wang,Tianrun Yuan,Juntong Chen,Yongkang Zhu,Fanhu Zeng,Xuanyu Zhu,Yige Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StepSTEM是一个研究生级别的多模态推理基准测试，包含283个STEM领域问题，通过严格的模态互补性设计和步骤级评估框架来评估MLLMs的跨模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs评估存在两个主要问题：1）模态冗余导致单模态捷径，2）只关注最终答案准确性而忽略推理过程。STEM领域因其高度可验证性成为理想的测试平台。

Method: 1）构建StepSTEM基准：通过严格筛选流程确保文本和视觉输入的严格互补性；2）提出步骤级评估框架：使用动态规划将预测推理步骤与多个参考解决方案对齐，支持纯文本链式思维和交错图像-文本推理。

Result: 实验表明当前MLLMs仍严重依赖文本推理，即使Gemini 3.1 Pro和Claude Opus 4.6也仅达到38.29%的准确率，显示出真正的跨模态STEM推理仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: StepSTEM为MLLMs的跨模态推理提供了细粒度评估基准，揭示了当前模型在跨模态STEM推理方面的局限性，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning abilities, yet evaluating their performance in specialized domains remains challenging. STEM reasoning is a particularly valuable testbed because it provides highly verifiable feedback, but existing benchmarks often permit unimodal shortcuts due to modality redundancy and focus mainly on final-answer accuracy, overlooking the reasoning process itself. To address this challenge, we introduce StepSTEM: a graduate-level benchmark of 283 problems across mathematics, physics, chemistry, biology, and engineering for fine-grained evaluation of cross-modal reasoning in MLLMs. StepSTEM is constructed through a rigorous curation pipeline that enforces strict complementarity between textual and visual inputs. We further propose a general step-level evaluation framework for both text-only chain-of-thought and interleaved image-text reasoning, using dynamic programming to align predicted reasoning steps with multiple reference solutions. Experiments across a wide range of models show that current MLLMs still rely heavily on textual reasoning, with even Gemini 3.1 Pro and Claude Opus 4.6 achieving only 38.29% accuracy. These results highlight substantial headroom for genuine cross-modal STEM reasoning and position StepSTEM as a benchmark for fine-grained evaluation of multimodal reasoning. Source code is available at https://github.com/lll-hhh/STEPSTEM.

</details>


### [84] [Face Anything: 4D Face Reconstruction from Any Image Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19702)
*Umut Kocasari,Simon Giebenhain,Richard Shaw,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于规范面部点预测的统一4D面部重建方法，通过将像素映射到共享规范空间，将动态重建和跟踪转化为规范重建问题，实现高精度深度估计和时间一致的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人脸重建面临非刚性变形、表情变化和视角变化同时发生的挑战，导致几何和对应关系估计存在显著模糊性。现有方法难以在单一模型中同时实现高精度重建和可靠跟踪。

Method: 提出规范面部点预测表示法，将每个像素映射到共享规范空间中的标准化面部坐标。使用基于Transformer的模型联合预测深度和规范面部坐标，通过多视角几何数据训练，将非刚性变形映射到规范空间。

Result: 在图像和视频基准测试中实现最先进性能：对应误差降低约3倍，推理速度更快，深度精度提高16%。能够实现准确深度估计、时间稳定重建、密集3D几何和鲁棒面部点跟踪。

Conclusion: 规范面部点预测是统一前馈4D面部重建的有效基础，通过将动态跟踪和重建转化为规范重建问题，在单一模型中实现了高保真度重建和可靠对应关系。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction and tracking of dynamic human faces from image sequences is challenging because non-rigid deformations, expression changes, and viewpoint variations occur simultaneously, creating significant ambiguity in geometry and correspondence estimation. We present a unified method for high-fidelity 4D facial reconstruction based on canonical facial point prediction, a representation that assigns each pixel a normalized facial coordinate in a shared canonical space. This formulation transforms dense tracking and dynamic reconstruction into a canonical reconstruction problem, enabling temporally consistent geometry and reliable correspondences within a single feed-forward model. By jointly predicting depth and canonical coordinates, our method enables accurate depth estimation, temporally stable reconstruction, dense 3D geometry, and robust facial point tracking within a single architecture. We implement this formulation using a transformer-based model that jointly predicts depth and canonical facial coordinates, trained using multi-view geometry data that non-rigidly warps into the canonical space. Extensive experiments on image and video benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across reconstruction and tracking tasks, achieving approximately 3$\times$ lower correspondence error and faster inference than prior dynamic reconstruction methods, while improving depth accuracy by 16%. These results highlight canonical facial point prediction as an effective foundation for unified feed-forward 4D facial reconstruction.

</details>


### [85] [SpanVLA: Efficient Action Bridging and Learning from Negative-Recovery Samples for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19710)
*Zewei Zhou,Ruining Yang,Xuewei,Qi,Yiluan Guo,Sherry X. Chen,Tao Feng,Kateryna Pistunova,Yishan Shen,Lili Su,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpanVLA：一种结合自回归推理和流匹配动作专家的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过高效桥接VLM的视觉推理指导来规划轨迹，显著降低推理延迟，并通过GRPO后训练提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在自动驾驶中存在两个主要问题：1) 使用自回归生成框架导致动作生成延迟高；2) 鲁棒性有限。需要一种既能利用VLM的世界知识和推理能力，又能高效生成动作并提升鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出SpanVLA框架，集成自回归推理模块和流匹配动作专家，通过高效桥接机制利用VLM的视觉和推理指导来规划未来轨迹，基于历史轨迹初始化进行条件化流匹配策略，显著降低推理时间。2) 提出基于GRPO的后训练方法，使VLA模型不仅能从正样本学习，还能学习避免典型负行为和恢复行为。3) 引入mReasoning数据集，专注于复杂推理场景和负恢复样本。

Result: 在NAVSIM（v1和v2）数据集上的大量实验表明SpanVLA模型具有竞争性性能。跨多样场景的定性结果突出了模型的规划性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpanVLA通过结合自回归推理和流匹配动作专家，有效解决了VLA模型在自动驾驶中的高延迟和鲁棒性有限问题，为自动驾驶提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising autonomous driving paradigm for leveraging world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, especially in long-tail scenarios. However, existing VLA models often struggle with the high latency in action generation using an autoregressive generation framework and exhibit limited robustness. In this paper, we propose SpanVLA, a novel end-to-end autonomous driving framework, integrating an autoregressive reasoning and a flow-matching action expert. First, SpanVLA introduces an efficient bridge to leverage the vision and reasoning guidance of VLM to efficiently plan future trajectories using a flow-matching policy conditioned on historical trajectory initialization, which significantly reduces inference time. Second, to further improve the performance and robustness of the SpanVLA model, we propose a GRPO-based post-training method to enable the VLA model not only to learn from positive driving samples but also to learn how to avoid the typical negative behaviors and learn recovery behaviors. We further introduce mReasoning, a new real-world driving reasoning dataset, focusing on complex, reasoning-demanding scenarios and negative-recovery samples. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM (v1 and v2) demonstrate the competitive performance of the SpanVLA model. Additionally, the qualitative results across diverse scenarios highlight the planning performance and robustness of our model.

</details>


### [86] [Generative Drifting for Conditional Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19736)
*Zirong Li,Siyuan Mei,Weiwen Wu,Andreas Maier,Lina Gölz,Yan Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GDM是一个生成漂移框架，将确定性医学图像预测重新表述为多目标学习问题，在保持一步推理的同时联合提升分布级合理性和患者特定保真度，特别针对3D医学成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件医学图像生成方法在平衡推理效率、患者特定保真度和分布级合理性方面面临挑战，特别是在高维3D医学成像中。需要一种能同时满足这些要求的方法。

Method: GDM采用生成漂移框架，通过吸引-排斥漂移最小化生成器推送与目标分布之间的差异。使用医学基础编码器构建多级特征库支持可靠的亲和度估计和漂移场计算，并通过共享输出空间中的梯度协调策略优化竞争目标间的平衡。

Result: 在MRI-to-CT合成和稀疏视图CT重建任务上，GDM在解剖保真度、定量可靠性、感知真实性和推理效率的平衡方面优于多种基线方法，包括GAN、流匹配、SDE生成模型和监督回归方法。

Conclusion: GDM为条件3D医学图像生成提供了一个实用有效的框架，能够更好地平衡推理效率、患者特定保真度和分布级合理性。

Abstract: Conditional medical image generation plays an important role in many clinically relevant imaging tasks. However, existing methods still face a fundamental challenge in balancing inference efficiency, patient-specific fidelity, and distribution-level plausibility, particularly in high-dimensional 3D medical imaging. In this work, we propose GDM, a generative drifting framework that reformulates deterministic medical image prediction as a multi-objective learning problem to jointly promote distribution-level plausibility and patient-specific fidelity while retaining one-step inference. GDM extends drifting to 3D medical imaging through an attractive-repulsive drift that minimizes the discrepancy between the generator pushforward and the target distribution. To enable stable drifting-based learning in 3D volumetric data, GDM constructs a multi-level feature bank from a medical foundation encoder to support reliable affinity estimation and drifting field computation across complementary global, local, and spatial representations. In addition, a gradient coordination strategy in the shared output space improves optimization balance under competing distribution-level and fidelity-oriented objectives. We evaluate the proposed framework on two representative tasks, MRI-to-CT synthesis and sparse-view CT reconstruction. Experimental results show that GDM consistently outperforms a wide range of baselines, including GAN-based, flow-matching-based, and SDE-based generative models, as well as supervised regression methods, while improving the balance among anatomical fidelity, quantitative reliability, perceptual realism, and inference efficiency. These findings suggest that GDM provides a practical and effective framework for conditional 3D medical image generation.

</details>


### [87] [AnyRecon: Arbitrary-View 3D Reconstruction with Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19747)
*Yutian Chen,Shi Guo,Renbiao Jin,Tianshuo Yang,Xin Cai,Yawen Luo,Mingxin Yang,Mulin Yu,Linning Xu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyRecon是一个用于任意无序稀疏输入的可扩展3D重建框架，通过持久全局场景记忆和几何感知条件策略，在保持显式几何控制的同时支持灵活的条件基数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图3D重建对于从随意拍摄中建模场景至关重要，但现有基于扩散的方法通常只基于一到两帧进行条件化，这限制了几何一致性，难以扩展到大型或多样化场景。

Method: 1) 通过前置捕获视图缓存构建持久全局场景记忆；2) 移除时间压缩以保持大视角变化下的帧级对应；3) 引入几何感知条件策略，通过显式3D几何记忆和几何驱动的捕获视图检索耦合生成与重建；4) 结合4步扩散蒸馏和上下文窗口稀疏注意力降低二次复杂度。

Result: 实验证明该方法能够在不规则输入、大视角间隙和长轨迹场景中实现鲁棒且可扩展的重建。

Conclusion: AnyRecon通过持久场景记忆和几何感知条件策略，解决了稀疏视图3D重建中的几何一致性和可扩展性问题，为大规模场景重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D reconstruction is essential for modeling scenes from casual captures, but remain challenging for non-generative reconstruction. Existing diffusion-based approaches mitigates this issues by synthesizing novel views, but they often condition on only one or two capture frames, which restricts geometric consistency and limits scalability to large or diverse scenes. We propose AnyRecon, a scalable framework for reconstruction from arbitrary and unordered sparse inputs that preserves explicit geometric control while supporting flexible conditioning cardinality. To support long-range conditioning, our method constructs a persistent global scene memory via a prepended capture view cache, and removes temporal compression to maintain frame-level correspondence under large viewpoint changes. Beyond better generative model, we also find that the interplay between generation and reconstruction is crucial for large-scale 3D scenes. Thus, we introduce a geometry-aware conditioning strategy that couples generation and reconstruction through an explicit 3D geometric memory and geometry-driven capture-view retrieval. To ensure efficiency, we combine 4-step diffusion distillation with context-window sparse attention to reduce quadratic complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate robust and scalable reconstruction across irregular inputs, large viewpoint gaps, and long trajectories.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Position: No Retroactive Cure for Infringement during Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18649)
*Satoru Utsunomiya,Masaru Isonuma,Junichiro Mori,Ichiro Sakata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文认为，生成式AI面临法律挑战时，事后缓解措施（如机器遗忘和推理时护栏）无法追溯性地解决非法获取和训练数据带来的责任问题，因为合规性取决于数据来源而非输出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI面临日益严峻的法律挑战，机器学习社区越来越依赖事后缓解措施来论证合规性。作者认为这种事后补救方法存在根本性缺陷，无法解决数据获取和训练阶段的非法性问题。

Method: 通过法律分析框架，从三个层面论证事后缓解措施的局限性：1) 未经授权的复制/摄取是完整的法律行为，模型权重作为固定副本保留了训练数据的表达价值；2) 合同和侵权/不正当竞争规则独立限制访问和使用；3) 受保护输入的价值可能持续存在于权重中，需要剥离不当得利。

Result: 论证表明事后缓解措施无法追溯性地治愈非法数据获取和训练带来的法律责任，因为合规性核心在于数据来源而非输出结果。

Conclusion: 需要从"事后净化"转向可验证的"事前流程合规"，确保数据获取和训练过程的合法性，而非依赖输出后的补救措施。

Abstract: As generative AI faces intensifying legal challenges, the machine learning community has increasingly relied on post-hoc mitigation -- especially machine unlearning and inference-time guardrails -- to argue for compliance. This paper argues that such post-hoc mitigation methods cannot retroactively cure liability from unlawful acquisition and training, because compliance hinges on data lineage, not the outputs. Our argument has three parts. First, unauthorized copying/ingestion can be a legally complete completed act, and model weights may operate as fixed copies that retain training-derived expressive value, making later filtering beside the point for infringement. Second, contract and tort/unfair-competition rules -- via licenses, terms of service, and anti-free-riding principles -- can independently restrict access and use, often bypassing copyright defenses (e.g., fair use or TDM exceptions). Third, since value from protected inputs can persist in weights, remedies such as unjust enrichment and disgorgement may require stripping gains and, in some cases, reaching the model itself. We therefore argue for a shift from Post-Hoc Sanitization to verifiable Ex-Ante Process Compliance.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Explicit Refusals: Soft-Failure Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18663)
*Wentao Zhang,Yan Zhuang,ZhuHang Zheng,Mingfei Zhang,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DEJA攻击框架，通过生成对抗性文档诱导RAG系统产生流畅但无信息量的软失效响应，而非显式拒绝或拒绝服务等硬失效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的干扰攻击通常导致显式拒绝或拒绝服务等易被检测的硬失效行为，需要研究更隐蔽的软失效威胁，即诱导系统产生流畅连贯但无信息量的响应，从而降低系统效用

Method: 提出欺骗性进化干扰攻击(DEJA)框架，采用黑盒攻击方式，利用LLM的安全对齐行为生成对抗性文档。通过进化优化过程，以细粒度答案效用评分(AUS)为指导，系统性地降低答案确定性同时保持高检索成功率

Result: 在多种RAG配置和基准数据集上的实验表明，DEJA能持续将响应导向低效用软失效，SASR超过79%而硬失效率低于15%，显著优于先前攻击。对抗性文档具有高隐蔽性，能规避基于困惑度的检测，抵抗查询改写，并能跨模型家族迁移到专有系统

Conclusion: DEJA攻击框架揭示了RAG系统中一种新的软失效威胁，通过利用LLM的安全对齐行为，能够以隐蔽方式降低系统效用，这对RAG系统的安全性和鲁棒性提出了新的挑战

Abstract: Existing jamming attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems typically induce explicit refusals or denial-of-service behaviors, which are conspicuous and easy to detect. In this work, we formalize a subtler availability threat, termed soft failure, which degrades system utility by inducing fluent and coherent yet non-informative responses rather than overt failures. We propose Deceptive Evolutionary Jamming Attack (DEJA), an automated black-box attack framework that generates adversarial documents to trigger such soft failures by exploiting safety-aligned behaviors of large language models. DEJA employs an evolutionary optimization process guided by a fine-grained Answer Utility Score (AUS), computed via an LLM-based evaluator, to systematically degrade the certainty of answers while maintaining high retrieval success. Extensive experiments across multiple RAG configurations and benchmark datasets show that DEJA consistently drives responses toward low-utility soft failures, achieving SASR above 79\% while keeping hard-failure rates below 15\%, significantly outperforming prior attacks. The resulting adversarial documents exhibit high stealth, evading perplexity-based detection and resisting query paraphrasing, and transfer across model families to proprietary systems without retargeting.

</details>


### [90] [TrEEStealer: Stealing Decision Trees via Enclave Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18716)
*Jonas Sander,Anja Rabich,Nick Mahling,Felix Maurer,Jonah Heller,Qifan Wang,Thomas Eisenbarth,David Oswald*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrEEStealer：针对TEE保护决策树的高保真提取攻击，利用TEE侧信道窃取模型，无需强API假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在敏感安全应用中广泛部署，模型提取攻击威胁MLaaS商业模式，而TEE作为硬件隔离机制被认为能限制黑盒攻击。现有DT提取攻击查询密集或依赖强假设，需要验证TEE的实际防护效果

Method: TrEEStealer利用TEE特定侧信道，结合控制流信息与被动信息跟踪，最大化每个查询的信息获取。针对AMD SEV使用SEV-Step框架和性能计数器，针对Intel SGX通过Branch-History-Register提取分支历史

Result: 在OpenCV、mlpack、emlearn三个流行库中发现漏洞，TrEEStealer相比先前攻击在效率和提取保真度上表现更优，确认TEE无法防护控制流泄漏

Conclusion: 建立了决策树提取的新SOTA，证明TEE无法有效防护控制流泄漏攻击，对TEE保护机器学习模型的安全性提出质疑

Abstract: Today, machine learning is widely applied in sensitive, security-related, and financially lucrative applications. Model extraction attacks undermine current business models where a model owner sells model access, e.g., via MLaaS APIs. Additionally, stolen models can enable powerful white-box attacks, facilitating privacy attacks on sensitive training data, and model evasion.
  In this paper, we focus on Decision Trees (DT), which are widely deployed in practice. Existing black-box extraction attacks for DTs are either query-intensive, make strong assumptions about the DT structure, or rely on rich API information. To limit attacks to the black-box setting, CPU vendors introduced Trusted Execution Environments (TEE) that use hardware-mechanisms to isolate workloads from external parties, e.g., MLaaS providers. We introduce TrEEStealer, a high-fidelity extraction attack for stealing TEE-protected DTs. TrEEStealer exploits TEE-specific side-channels to steal DTs efficiently and without strong assumptions about the API output or DT structure. The extraction efficacy stems from a novel algorithm that maximizes the information derived from each query by coupling Control-Flow Information (CFI) with passive information tracking. We use two primitives to acquire CFI: for AMD SEV, we follow previous work using the SEV-Step framework and performance counters. For Intel SGX, we reproduce prior findings on current Xeon 6 CPUs and construct a new primitive to efficiently extract the branch history of inference runs through the Branch-History-Register.
  We found corresponding vulnerabilities in three popular libraries: OpenCV, mlpack, and emlearn. We show that TrEEStealer achieves superior efficiency and extraction fidelity compared to prior attacks. Our work establishes a new state-of-the-art for DT extraction and confirms that TEEs fail to protect against control-flow leakage.

</details>


### [91] [Temporal UI State Inconsistency in Desktop GUI Agents: Formalizing and Defending Against TOCTOU Attacks on Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18860)
*Wenpeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GUI智能体在桌面控制中引入新的安全漏洞——观察-执行间隙导致TOCTOU攻击窗口，研究者提出视觉原子性违规概念和三种攻击原语，并设计了三层防御机制PUSV进行防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体通过截图-点击循环控制桌面计算机时，观察与执行之间存在时间间隙（平均6.51秒），这创建了TOCTOU攻击窗口，使得无权限攻击者可以在此期间操纵UI状态，从而劫持智能体的操作。

Method: 1. 形式化视觉原子性违规概念；2. 识别三种具体攻击原语：通知覆盖劫持、窗口焦点操纵、Web DOM注入；3. 提出预执行UI状态验证(PUSV)防御机制，包含三层：点击目标掩码像素SSIM(L1)、全局截图差异(L2a)、X窗口快照差异(L2b)。

Result: 1. 窗口焦点操纵攻击原语实现100%动作重定向成功率且观察时无视觉证据；2. PUSV在180次对抗试验中实现100%动作拦截率，零误报，开销<0.1秒；3. 对DOM注入攻击，PUSV存在结构性盲点（约0%拦截率）；4. 单一PUSV层无法实现完全覆盖，验证了分层设计的必要性。

Conclusion: GUI智能体存在严重的TOCTOU安全漏洞，PUSV三层防御机制能有效防护视觉攻击原语，但对DOM注入等无视觉痕迹的攻击存在局限，需要未来操作系统+DOM深度防御架构的协同防护。

Abstract: GUI agents that control desktop computers via screenshot-and-click loops introduce a new class of vulnerability: the observation-to-action gap (mean 6.51 s on real OSWorld workloads) creates a Time-Of-Check, Time-Of-Use (TOCTOU) window during which an unprivileged attacker can manipulate the UI state. We formalize this as a Visual Atomicity Violation and characterize three concrete attack primitives: (A) Notification Overlay Hijack, (B) Window Focus Manipulation, and (C) Web DOM Injection. Primitive B, the closest desktop analog to Android Action Rebinding, achieves 100% action-redirection success rate with zero visual evidence at the observation time. We propose Pre-execution UI State Verification (PUSV), a lightweight three-layer defense that re-verifies the UI state immediately before each action dispatch: masked pixel SSIM at the click target (L1), global screenshot diff (L2a), and X Window snapshot diff (L2b). PUSV achieves 100% Action Interception Rate across 180 adversarial trials (135 Primitive A + 45 Primitive B) with zero false positives and < 0.1 s overhead. Against Primitive C (zero-visual-footprint DOM injection), PUSV reveals a structural blind spot (~0% AIR), motivating future OS+DOM defense-in-depth architectures. No single PUSV layer alone achieves full coverage; different primitives require different detection signals, validating the layered design.

</details>


### [92] [API Security Based on Automatic OpenAPI Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19471)
*Yarin Levi,Ran Dubin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MRG是一种无监督的HTTP REST API建模与安全方法，通过分析真实流量自动生成OpenAPI文档，实现实时更新、可视化解释和异常检测，在检测性能上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态微服务环境中HTTP REST API缺乏自动化建模和安全监控方法，现有方法需要先验知识或标注数据，难以适应API的持续演化和实时安全需求。

Method: MRG采用三阶段流程：训练阶段从真实流量无监督学习API结构；更新阶段实时适应API变化；检测阶段使用图基验证和深度自编码器进行异常检测，包括畸形请求、结构偏差和注入攻击检测。

Result: 相比HRAL和FT-ANN等先进方法，MRG召回率提升11.4%，推理速度快20倍以上，在多种API层攻击检测中达到100%精确度，支持实时更新和可解释可视化。

Conclusion: MRG为动态微服务环境提供了一种完全自动化、高效的API可见性、模式推断和异常检测管道，无需人工调优或标注数据，能够适应API的持续演化。

Abstract: This paper presents Map Reduce Graph (MRG), a novel unsupervised method for modeling and securing HTTP REST APIs. MRG learns API structure from real-world traffic without prior knowledge or labels, automatically generating OpenAPI-compliant documentation by reconstructing routes, methods, and parameter formats. MRG enables real-time updates, explainable visualization, and anomaly detection, helping identify undocumented or evolving behaviors. It detects malformed requests, structural deviations, and injection attacks using graph-based validation and a deep autoencoder for payload analysis. Compared to state-of-the-art methods like HRAL and FT-ANN, MRG achieves up to 11.4% higher recall, over 20 times faster inference, and perfect precision (100%) on multiple API-layer attacks. Designed for dynamic microservice environments, MRG operates in three phases - training, updating, and detection - and integrates smoothly with observability and security tools. This work contributes a fully automated, efficient pipeline for real-time API visibility, schema inference, and anomaly detection without manual tuning or labeled data.

</details>


### [93] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Obfuscated XSS Payloads for Machine Learning-Based Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19526)
*Divyesh Gabbireddy,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出使用大语言模型生成和评估混淆XSS载荷的结构化流程，通过浏览器运行时评估确保行为有效性，但结果显示当前LLM在保持运行时行为方面仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XSS攻击中混淆技术能改变载荷表面形式但保持恶意行为，这使得传统和基于机器学习的检测系统难以可靠识别。现有生成混淆载荷的方法强调语法多样性，但无法确保生成样本的行为有效性。

Method: 提出结构化流程：结合确定性转换技术和基于LLM的生成方法，使用基于浏览器的运行时评估程序在受控执行环境中比较载荷行为。通过微调行为保持的源-目标混淆对来改进模型。

Result: 未调优基线语言模型的运行时行为匹配率为0.15，在行为保持的源-目标混淆对上微调后匹配率提高到0.22。添加生成的载荷不会改善检测性能，但经过行为过滤的生成样本可以纳入而不显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 研究展示了生成模型在对抗性安全数据生成中的潜力和局限性，强调了运行时行为检查对于提高下游检测系统生成数据质量的重要性。当前LLM在生成保持运行时行为的混淆方面仍有困难。

Abstract: Cross-site scripting (XSS) remains a persistent web security vulnerability, especially because obfuscation can change the surface form of a malicious payload while preserving its behavior. These transformations make it difficult for traditional and machine learning-based detection systems to reliably identify attacks. Existing approaches for generating obfuscated payloads often emphasize syntactic diversity, but they do not always ensure that the generated samples remain behaviorally valid. This paper presents a structured pipeline for generating and evaluating obfuscated XSS payloads using large language models (LLMs). The pipeline combines deterministic transformation techniques with LLM-based generation and uses a browser- based runtime evaluation procedure to compare payload behavior in a controlled execution environment. This allows generated samples to be assessed through observable runtime behavior rather than syntactic similarity alone. In the evaluation, an untuned baseline language model achieves a runtime behavior match rate of 0.15, while fine-tuning on behavior-preserving source-target obfuscation pairs improves the match rate to 0.22. Although this represents a measurable improvement, the results show that current LLMs still struggle to generate obfuscations that preserve observed runtime behavior. A downstream classifier evaluation further shows that adding generated payloads does not improve detection performance in this setting, although behavior- filtered generated samples can be incorporated without materially degrading performance. Overall, the study demonstrates both the promise and the limits of applying generative models to adversarial security data generation and emphasizes the importance of runtime behavior checks in improving the quality of generated data for downstream detection systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Fast estimation of Gaussian mixture components via centering and singular value thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19091)
*Huan Qing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于奇异值分解的简单高斯混合模型分量数估计方法，无需迭代拟合或似然计算，在高维、多分量、严重类别不平衡场景下仍能一致恢复真实分量数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无监督学习中估计分量数的基本挑战，特别是在高维数据、多分量或严重不平衡分量大小的情况下。传统方法通常需要迭代拟合、似然计算或先验知识，计算复杂且在高维不平衡场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出一种简单的估计器：首先对数据进行中心化，然后计算中心化矩阵的奇异值，最后统计超过特定阈值的奇异值数量。该方法无需迭代拟合、似然计算或分量数先验知识。

Result: 在分量中心满足温和分离条件下，该估计器能一致恢复真实分量数。结果适用于高维设置（维度可远大于样本量），且当分量数增长到维度与样本量的较小值时仍有效，即使存在严重的不平衡。计算效率极高，例如能在1分钟内处理100维的1000万样本。

Conclusion: 该方法为高斯混合模型分量数估计提供了一种简单、快速且理论保证的解决方案，特别适用于高维、多分量和严重类别不平衡的挑战性场景，实验研究证实了其准确性。

Abstract: Estimating the number of components is a fundamental challenge in unsupervised learning, particularly when dealing with high-dimensional data with many components or severely imbalanced component sizes. This paper addresses this challenge for classical Gaussian mixture models. The proposed estimator is simple: center the data, compute the singular values of the centered matrix, and count those above a threshold. No iterative fitting, no likelihood calculation, and no prior knowledge of the number of components are required. We prove that, under a mild separation condition on the component centers, the estimator consistently recovers the true number of components. The result holds in high-dimensional settings where the dimension can be much larger than the sample size. It also holds when the number of components grows to the smaller of the dimension and the sample size, even under severe imbalance among component sizes. Computationally, the method is extremely fast: for example, it processes ten million samples in one hundred dimensions within one minute. Extensive experimental studies confirm its accuracy in challenging settings such as high dimensionality, many components, and severe class imbalance.

</details>


### [95] [Analytical Extraction of Conditional Sobol' Indices via Basis Decomposition of Polynomial Chaos Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19165)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于预训练全局多项式混沌展开（PCE）模型的条件Sobol'指数解析计算方法，通过利用PCE基函数的张量积特性，将条件敏感性分析转化为纯代数后处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定性量化中，评估特定条件下的敏感性度量（如条件Sobol'指数）对于具有参数化响应的系统（如空间场或变化运行条件）至关重要。传统方法通常依赖于逐点建模，计算成本高且可能在参数空间中缺乏一致性。

Method: 利用预训练全局PCE模型的基函数张量积特性，将全局展开重构为一组依赖于条件变量的解析系数场。基于条件概率测度下正交性的保持，推导出条件方差和Sobol'指数的闭式表达式。

Result: 数值基准测试表明，所提方法确保了物理一致性，并在数值鲁棒性和计算效率方面优于传统的逐点方法。该方法将条件敏感性分析转化为纯代数后处理步骤，无需重复建模或额外采样。

Conclusion: 对于预训练的全局PCE模型，条件Sobol'指数已内嵌于其基函数中。通过利用PCE基的张量积特性，可以推导出条件敏感性的解析表达式，显著提高计算效率并确保物理一致性。

Abstract: In uncertainty quantification, evaluating sensitivity measures under specific conditions (i.e., conditional Sobol' indices) is essential for systems with parameterized responses, such as spatial fields or varying operating conditions. Traditional approaches often rely on point-wise modeling, which is computationally expensive and may lack consistency across the parameter space. This paper demonstrates that for a pre-trained global Polynomial Chaos Expansion (PCE) model, the analytical conditional Sobol' indices are inherently embedded within its basis functions. By leveraging the tensor-product property of PCE bases, we reformulate the global expansion into a set of analytical coefficient fields that depend on the conditioning variables. Based on the preservation of orthogonality under conditional probability measures, we derive closed-form expressions for conditional variances and Sobol' indices. This framework bypasses the need for repetitive modeling or additional sampling, transforming conditional sensitivity analysis into a purely algebraic post-processing step. Numerical benchmarks indicate that the proposed method ensures physical coherence and offers superior numerical robustness and computational efficiency compared to conventional point-wise approaches.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Stochastic Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19352)
*Rohit Chaudhuri*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该研究将随机干预应用于多治疗场景，旨在找到能最大化期望潜在结果的最优治疗分布，其中治疗数量可随样本量变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为各种治疗效应提供新颖的总结方法，指导实践者做出更好的干预选择决策，特别是在治疗数量可变的复杂场景中

Method: 应用随机干预方法，在治疗数量随n变化的设定下，寻找能产生高期望潜在结果的最优治疗分布

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体结果，但该方法旨在提供治疗效应的新颖总结框架

Conclusion: 随机干预方法可用于确定最优治疗分布，为实践者提供决策指导，特别是在治疗选项动态变化的复杂环境中

Abstract: This article discusses the application of stochastic intervention to find the optimal treatment distribution yielding a high value of expected potential outcome under the setting where the number of treatments is allowed to vary with $n$. The primary motivation is to obtain a novel summarization of the effect of various treatments which would guide practitioners towards better decision regarding which intervention to choose.

</details>


### [97] [Asymptotic e-processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19353)
*Pierre-François Massiani,Sebastian Schulze,Mattes Mollenhauer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文引入渐近e过程概念，用于近似e过程以处理模型误设和估计误差，推导了渐近Ville不等式并研究其性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序贯假设检验的实际应用中，由于模型误设或估计误差，通常只能从观测中近似构造e变量。需要理论框架来处理这种近似情况。

Method: 引入渐近e过程概念，即双重索引随机过程(E_{m,n})，通过近似参数m→∞的极限行为来近似监控时间n的e过程。推导渐近Ville不等式，研究渐近e过程与渐近上鞅的联系，并提供从渐近e变量构造渐近e过程的方法。

Result: 建立了渐近e过程的理论框架，推导了渐近Ville不等式，该不等式在由过程近似质量决定的时间范围r_m内，一致地界定了(E_{m,n})超过某个阈值的游走概率。展示了渐近e过程与渐近上鞅的联系，并提供了构造示例。

Conclusion: 渐近e过程为处理序贯假设检验中的近似e变量提供了理论工具，通过渐近Ville不等式确保了近似过程的统计有效性，扩展了e过程理论在实际应用中的适用范围。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of an asymptotic e-process, which is a doubly indexed stochastic process $(E_{m,n})_{m,n\in\mathbb{N}}$ that approximates an e-process with monitoring time $n$ in terms of a suitable limiting behavior for an approximation parameter $m\to \infty$. This theory is motivated by practical applications in sequential hypothesis testing, in which e-variables can only be constructed approximately from observations due to model misspecification or estimation errors. We derive an asymptotic version of Ville's inequality, which bounds excursion probabilities of $(E_{m,n})_{m,n\in\mathbb{N}}$ over some threshold uniformly over $n$ up to a time horizon $r_m$ that is determined by the quality of process approximation over $m$. We investigate properties of asymptotic e-processes, their connections to asymptotic supermartingales, and provide examples of how they can be constructed from asymptotic e-variables.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Compile to Compress: Boosting Formal Theorem Provers by Compiler Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18587)
*Guchan Li,Rui Tian,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习-精炼框架的高效定理证明方法，利用编译器将多样证明尝试映射到结构化失败模式的压缩特性，通过局部修正错误避免长历史记录成本，显著提升基础证明器的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在形式定理证明中表现出潜力，但最先进性能通常需要通过大规模展开或扩展上下文窗口来获得，这带来了过高的测试时计算成本。为了解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，作者利用了形式验证中的一个信息结构：编译器将大量多样的证明尝试映射到紧凑的结构化失败模式集合。

Method: 引入学习-精炼框架，利用编译器压缩特性进行高效学习和证明探索。执行树搜索，根据明确的验证器反馈局部修正错误，从而避免累积长证明尝试历史的成本。该方法通过利用验证器反馈来指导证明过程的精炼。

Result: 广泛评估表明，该方法在不同规模上持续增强了基础证明器的推理能力。在PutnamBench上，该方法在可比较的测试时预算下，在公开报告的约8B和约32B参数模型中实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为下一代验证器引导的推理提供了一个可扩展的范式，通过利用编译器压缩特性和局部错误修正，有效解决了形式定理证明中的可扩展性瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in formal theorem proving, yet state-of-the-art performance often necessitates prohibitive test-time compute via massive roll-outs or extended context windows. In this work, we address this scalability bottleneck by exploiting an informative structure in formal verification: the observation that compilers map a vast space of diverse proof attempts to a compact set of structured failure modes. We introduce a learning-to-refine framework that leverages this compression to perform efficient learning and proof exploration. We perform tree search that corrects errors locally conditioned on explicit verifier feedback, thereby circumventing the costs associated with accumulating a long history of proof attempts. Extensive evaluations show that our method consistently amplifies the reasoning capabilities of base provers across varying scales. Notably, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on PutnamBench among publicly reported $\sim$8B and $\sim$32B parameter models under comparable test-time budgets, offering a scalable paradigm for next-generation verifier-guided reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [Easy Samples Are All You Need: Self-Evolving LLMs via Data-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18639)
*Zhiyin Yu,Bo Zhang,Qibin Hou,Zhonghai Wu,Xiao Luo,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyRL提出了一种基于认知学习理论的新方法，通过整合简单标注数据的可靠知识转移和渐进式分治策略来处理困难未标注数据，显著减少了标注成本并提升了LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的强化学习方法存在标注成本高、模型坍塌或奖励黑客等问题，性能远未达到满意水平。需要一种既能减少标注成本又能避免这些问题的有效方法。

Method: 1) 使用少量标注数据进行监督RL初始化预热模型；2) 对困难未标注数据实施分治伪标注策略，结合基于一致性的低不确定性案例选择和基于反思的中等不确定性案例解决；3) 难度渐进的自训练，通过迭代伪标注和RL进一步增强模型推理能力。

Result: 在数学和科学基准测试中，EasyRL仅使用10%的简单标注数据，就能持续超越最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: EasyRL提供了一个统一的自进化框架，促进了LLM的数据高效后训练，通过模拟人类认知获取曲线，有效解决了现有RL方法的局限性。

Abstract: Previous LLMs-based RL studies typically follow either supervised learning with high annotation costs, or unsupervised paradigms using voting or entropy-based rewards. However, their performance remains far from satisfactory due to the substantial annotation cost and issues such as model collapse or reward hacking. To address these issues, we introduce a new perspective inspired by cognitive learning theory and propose a novel approach called EasyRL. The core of EasyRL is to simulate the human cognitive acquisition curve by integrating reliable knowledge transfer from easy labeled data with a progressive divide-and-conquer strategy that tackles increasingly difficult unlabeled data. Specifically, we initialize a warm-up model using supervised RL with few-shot labeled data. This is followed by a divide-and-conquer pseudo-labeling strategy on difficult unlabeled data, combining consistency-based selection for low-uncertainty cases and reflection-based resolution for medium-uncertainty cases. Finally, difficulty-progressive self-training with iterative pseudo-labeling and RL further strengthens the model's reasoning capability. EasyRL provides a unified self-evolving framework that facilitates data-efficient post-training of LLMs. Experimental results on mathematical and scientific benchmarks demonstrate that EasyRL, using only 10% of easy labeled data, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Curiosity-Critic: Cumulative Prediction Error Improvement as a Tractable Intrinsic Reward for World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18701)
*Vin Bhaskara,Haicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Curiosity-Critic：一种新的内在奖励方法，基于世界模型累积预测误差的改进，而非单步预测误差，通过在线学习的状态转移渐近误差基线来区分可学习与随机转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预测误差的好奇心奖励方法只关注当前转移的预测误差，忽略了世界模型在所有访问转移上的累积预测误差。这导致探索效率低下，无法有效区分可学习的转移（认知不确定性）和随机转移（偶然不确定性）。

Method: 提出Curiosity-Critic方法，将内在奖励建立在累积目标函数的改进上，可简化为当前预测误差与当前状态转移渐近误差基线的差值。通过一个与世界模型协同训练的批评器在线估计该基线，批评器回归单个标量值，在世界模型饱和前就能收敛，从而将探索导向可学习的转移。

Result: 在随机网格世界实验中，Curiosity-Critic在收敛速度和最终世界模型准确性方面均优于基于预测误差和访问计数的基线方法。该方法能有效区分认知不确定性（可减少的）和偶然不确定性（不可减少的）预测误差。

Conclusion: Curiosity-Critic通过基于累积预测误差改进的内在奖励框架，提供了一种更有效的探索策略。该方法将先前的预测误差好奇心公式统一为特定基线近似的特例，并能在线分离认知和偶然不确定性，提高探索效率。

Abstract: Local prediction-error-based curiosity rewards focus on the current transition without considering the world model's cumulative prediction error across all visited transitions. We introduce Curiosity-Critic, which grounds its intrinsic reward in the improvement of this cumulative objective, and show that it reduces to a tractable per-step form: the difference between the current prediction error and the asymptotic error baseline of the current state transition. We estimate this baseline online with a learned critic co-trained alongside the world model; regressing a single scalar, the critic converges well before the world model saturates, redirecting exploration toward learnable transitions without oracle knowledge of the noise floor. The reward is higher for learnable transitions and collapses toward the baseline for stochastic ones, effectively separating epistemic (reducible) from aleatoric (irreducible) prediction error online. Prior prediction-error curiosity formulations, from Schmidhuber (1991) to learned-feature-space variants, emerge as special cases corresponding to specific approximations of this baseline. Experiments on a stochastic grid world show that Curiosity-Critic outperforms prediction-error and visitation-count baselines in convergence speed and final world model accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Coefficients: Forecast-Necessity Testing for Interpretable Causal Discovery in Nonlinear Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18751)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于预测必要性而非系数大小的非线性时间序列模型因果相关性评估框架，通过系统边消融和预测比较来测试候选因果关系是否对准确预测是必需的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性机器学习模型越来越多地用于发现时间序列数据中的因果关系，但其输出的解释仍不清晰。特别是正则化神经自回归模型产生的因果分数常被当作回归系数的类比，导致对统计显著性的误导性主张。

Method: 提出了一个基于系统边消融和预测比较的可解释评估框架，测试候选因果关系是否对准确预测是必需的。以神经加性向量自回归为案例研究模型，应用于139个国家民主发展指标的多变量时间序列面板数据。

Result: 具有相似因果分数的关系在预测必要性上可能存在显著差异，这归因于冗余性、时间持久性和特定体制效应。预测必要性测试支持更可靠的因果推理。

Conclusion: 预测必要性测试为应用AI系统中的可靠因果推理提供了支持，并为高风险领域中非线性时间序列模型的解释提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Nonlinear machine-learning models are increasingly used to discover causal relationships in time-series data, yet the interpretation of their outputs remains poorly understood. In particular, causal scores produced by regularized neural autoregressive models are often treated as analogues of regression coefficients, leading to misleading claims of statistical significance.
  In this paper, we argue that causal relevance in nonlinear time-series models should be evaluated through forecast necessity rather than coefficient magnitude, and we present a practical evaluation procedure for doing so. We present an interpretable evaluation framework based on systematic edge ablation and forecast comparison, which tests whether a candidate causal relationship is required for accurate prediction. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a case study model, we apply this framework to a real-world case study of democratic development, modeled as a multivariate time series of panel data - democracy indicators across 139 countries. We show that relationships with similar causal scores can differ dramatically in their predictive necessity due to redundancy, temporal persistence, and regime-specific effects.
  Our results demonstrate how forecast-necessity testing supports more reliable causal reasoning in applied AI systems and provides practical guidance for interpreting nonlinear time-series models in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [102] [Handling and Interpreting Missing Modalities in Patient Clinical Trajectories via Autoregressive Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18753)
*Andrew Wang,Ellie Pavlick,Ritambhara Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将临床诊断重构为自回归序列建模任务，利用大语言模型的因果解码器建模患者多模态轨迹，通过缺失感知对比预训练整合缺失模态，在MIMIC-IV和eICU基准上超越基线，并通过可解释性技术分析模态缺失影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态机器学习面临训练和部署中模态缺失的挑战，临床数据集具有时间性和模态稀疏性，需要在保持模型可解释性的同时捕捉预测信号。

Method: 1. 将临床诊断重构为自回归序列建模任务，使用大语言模型的因果解码器建模患者多模态轨迹；2. 引入缺失感知对比预训练目标，在共享潜在空间中整合具有缺失的多模态数据；3. 使用基于Transformer的架构进行自回归序列建模。

Result: 1. 自回归序列建模在MIMIC-IV和eICU微调基准上超越基线方法；2. 可解释性分析显示，移除模态会导致模型行为发散，而对比预训练能够缓解这种影响；3. 开发了能够分析和处理模态缺失的框架。

Conclusion: 通过将临床诊断抽象为序列建模并解释患者轨迹，开发了一个能够分析和处理模态缺失的框架，同时满足了安全、透明的临床AI的基本要求。

Abstract: An active challenge in developing multimodal machine learning (ML) models for healthcare is handling missing modalities during training and deployment. As clinical datasets are inherently temporal and sparse in terms of modality presence, capturing the underlying predictive signal via diagnostic multimodal ML models while retaining model explainability remains an ongoing challenge. In this work, we address this by re-framing clinical diagnosis as an autoregressive sequence modeling task, utilizing causal decoders from large language models (LLMs) to model a patient's multimodal trajectory. We first introduce a missingness-aware contrastive pre-training objective that integrates multiple modalities in datasets with missingness in a shared latent space. We then show that autoregressive sequence modeling with transformer-based architectures outperforms baselines on the MIMIC-IV and eICU fine-tuning benchmarks. Finally, we use interpretability techniques to move beyond performance boosts and find that across various patient stays, removing modalities leads to divergent behavior that our contrastive pre-training mitigates. By abstracting clinical diagnosis as sequence modeling and interpreting patient stay trajectories, we develop a framework to profile and handle missing modalities while addressing the canonical desideratum of safe, transparent clinical AI.

</details>


### [103] [Towards Understanding the Robustness of Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18756)
*Ahson Saiyed,Sabrina Sadiekh,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAE增强LLM防御：通过在推理时向transformer残差流注入预训练稀疏自编码器，显著降低基于优化的越狱攻击成功率，最高可达5倍降低，同时减少跨模型攻击可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型对基于优化的越狱攻击仍然脆弱，这些攻击利用内部梯度结构。虽然稀疏自编码器广泛用于可解释性，但其在鲁棒性方面的应用潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 在推理时向transformer残差流注入预训练稀疏自编码器，不修改模型权重或阻断梯度。在四个模型家族（Gemma、LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上测试，使用两种白盒攻击（GCG、BEAST）和三个黑盒基准进行评估。

Result: SAE增强模型相比未防御基线，越狱攻击成功率最高降低5倍，并减少跨模型攻击可迁移性。参数消融显示：1）L0稀疏度与攻击成功率呈单调剂量-响应关系；2）层依赖的防御-效用权衡，中间层在鲁棒性和清洁性能间达到平衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持表示瓶颈假说：稀疏投影重塑了越狱攻击所利用的优化几何结构。SAE集成提供了一种有前景的防御策略，在保持模型功能的同时增强对抗攻击鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to optimization-based jailbreak attacks that exploit internal gradient structure. While Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for interpretability, their robustness implications remain underexplored. We present a study of integrating pretrained SAEs into transformer residual streams at inference time, without modifying model weights or blocking gradients. Across four model families (Gemma, LLaMA, Mistral, Qwen) and two strong white-box attacks (GCG, BEAST) plus three black-box benchmarks, SAE-augmented models achieve up to a 5x reduction in jailbreak success rate relative to the undefended baseline and reduce cross-model attack transferability. Parametric ablations reveal (i) a monotonic dose-response relationship between L0 sparsity and attack success rate, and (ii) a layer-dependent defense-utility tradeoff, where intermediate layers balance robustness and clean performance. These findings are consistent with a representational bottleneck hypothesis: sparse projection reshapes the optimization geometry exploited by jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [104] [Streaming Structured Inference with Flash-SemiCRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18780)
*Benjamin K. Johnson,Thomas Goralski,Ayush Semwal,Hui Shen,H. Josh Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-SemiCRF：通过前缀和查找、流式前向-后向传递和零中心累积分数等技术，解决了半马尔可夫条件随机场在长序列和大标签集上的内存瓶颈问题，实现了精确的片段级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半CRF实现需要存储巨大的边势能张量，其大小随序列长度、最大片段长度和标签数量增长，在语音级状态空间下变得不可行，在基因组尺度（序列长度超过10万位置）下更是难以处理。这种内存瓶颈限制了精确片段级推理在长序列和大标签集上的应用。

Method: 1. 用前缀和查找替代存储的边张量，将内存占用减少与片段长度和标签数量乘积成比例的量级；2. 采用带检查点边界归一化的流式前向-后向传递，保持工作内存与序列长度呈亚线性关系，同时保留精确梯度；3. 使用零中心累积分数控制数值漂移，并在标签不平衡下诱导自适应持续时间先验。这些技术集成到Flash-SemiCRF，一个融合的Triton内核中。

Result: Flash-SemiCRF能够在以前难以处理的问题规模上实现精确的半CRF推理，解决了内存瓶颈问题，使精确片段级推理能够应用于长序列和大标签集场景。

Conclusion: 通过创新的内存优化技术，Flash-SemiCRF突破了半CRF在长序列和大标签集上的计算限制，为精确片段级推理在基因组学等大规模序列分析领域的应用提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-Markov Conditional Random Fields (semi-CRFs) assign labels to segments of a sequence rather than to individual positions, enabling exact inference over segment-level features and principled uncertainty estimates at their boundaries. However, existing implementations must materialize a large edge potential tensor whose size grows with sequence length, maximum segment length, and label count, becoming prohibitive for speech-scale state spaces and intractable at genomic scales where sequences can exceed 100,000 positions. This memory bottleneck has limited the adoption of exact segment-level inference for long sequences and large label sets. We identify that the core inefficiency is materializing edge potentials that can instead be evaluated on-the-fly from a compact prefix-sum array, and make several improvements. First, replacing the stored edge tensor with prefix-sum lookup reduces the memory footprint by a factor proportional to the product of segment length and label count. Second, a streaming forward-backward pass with checkpoint-boundary normalization keeps working memory sublinear in sequence length while preserving exact gradients. Third, zero-centered cumulative scores control numerical drift and induce an adaptive duration prior under label imbalance. We integrate these ideas into Flash-SemiCRF, a fused Triton kernel that enables exact semi-CRF inference on previously intractable problem sizes. Available at https://github.com/biobenkj/flash-semicrf.

</details>


### [105] [Efficient Mixture-of-Experts LLM Inference with Apple Silicon NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18788)
*Afsara Benazir,Felix Xiaozhu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NPUMoE是一个运行时推理引擎，通过将MoE LLMs的静态密集计算卸载到Apple Neural Engine，同时保留CPU/GPU回退路径处理动态操作，显著提升Apple Silicon上MoE推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLMs通过稀疏激活提高推理效率，但在NPU上存在三个挑战：专家路由不可预测导致动态张量形状与NPU形状特定约束冲突；不规则算子（如top-k、scatter/gather）不友好于NPU；启动许多小型专家内核会产生大量调度和同步开销。NPU旨在从CPU和GPU卸载AI计算，本文目标是实现MoE推理的此类卸载，特别是在长上下文工作负载消耗大量系统资源的预填充阶段。

Method: NPUMoE使用离线校准来估计专家容量和流行度，驱动三个关键技术：(1) 专家容量的静态层级以处理动态专家路由；(2) 分组专家执行以缓解NPU并发限制；(3) 负载感知的专家计算图驻留以减少CPU-NPU同步开销。系统将密集静态计算卸载到NPU，同时保留CPU/GPU回退路径处理动态操作。

Result: 在Apple M系列设备上使用三个代表性MoE LLMs和四个长上下文工作负载的实验表明，NPUMoE始终优于基线，通过有效的NPU卸载，延迟降低1.32x-5.55x，能效提高1.81x-7.37x，CPU周期使用减少1.78x-5.54x。

Conclusion: NPUMoE成功解决了MoE LLMs在Apple Silicon NPU上的执行挑战，通过智能的静态-动态计算分离和优化策略，实现了显著的性能提升和资源效率改进，为移动和边缘设备上的高效MoE推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Apple Neural Engine (ANE) is a dedicated neural processing unit (NPU) present in every Apple Silicon chip. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs improve inference efficiency via sparse activation but are challenging for NPUs in three ways: expert routing is unpredictable and introduces dynamic tensor shapes that conflict with the shape-specific constraints of NPUs; several irregular operators, e.g., top-k, scatter/gather, etc., are not NPU-friendly; and launching many small expert kernels incurs substantial dispatch and synchronization overhead. NPUs are designed to offload AI compute from CPU and GPU; our goal is to enable such offloading for MoE inference, particularly during prefill, where long-context workloads consume substantial system resources.
  This paper presents NPUMoE, a runtime inference engine that accelerates MoE execution on Apple Silicon by offloading dense, static computation to NPU, while preserving a CPU/GPU fallback path for dynamic operations. NPUMoE uses offline calibration to estimate expert capacity and popularity that drives three key techniques: (1) Static tiers for expert capacity to address dynamic expert routing (2) Grouped expert execution to mitigate NPU concurrency limits (3) Load-aware expert compute graph residency to reduce CPU-NPU synchronization overhead. Experiments on Apple M-series devices using three representative MoE LLMs and four long-context workloads show that NPUMoE consistently outperforms baselines, reducing latency by 1.32x-5.55x, improving energy efficiency by 1.81x-7.37x, and reducing CPU-cycle usage by 1.78x-5.54x through effective NPU offloading.

</details>


### [106] [HELM: Harness-Enhanced Long-horizon Memory for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18791)
*Zijian Zeng,Fei Ding,Huiming Yang,Xianwei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELM框架通过记忆模块、状态验证器和控制器解决VLA模型在长时程任务中的系统性问题，显著提升任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型在短时程任务上表现良好，但在长时程操作任务中存在系统性失败。研究发现仅扩展上下文长度无法解决这一问题，而是存在三个执行循环缺陷：记忆缺口、验证缺口和恢复缺口。

Method: 提出HELM框架，包含三个组件：1) 情景记忆模块(EMM)，通过CLIP索引关键帧检索任务历史；2) 学习型状态验证器(SV)，从观察、动作、子目标和记忆条件上下文中预测动作失败；3) 控制器(HC)，执行回滚和重新规划。状态验证器是核心学习贡献。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG上，HELM将任务成功率从OpenVLA的58.4%提升到81.5%，提升23.1个百分点。仅扩展上下文窗口到H=32仅带来5.4个百分点的提升，相同预算的LoRA适配仍比HELM低12.2个百分点。在CALVIN上也显著提升长时程性能，并在受控扰动下大幅提升恢复成功率。

Conclusion: HELM框架通过解决VLA模型在长时程任务中的三个执行循环缺陷，显著提升了模型性能。状态验证器的有效性关键依赖于情景记忆的访问。研究还发布了LIBERO-Recovery作为评估长时程操作中失败恢复的扰动注入协议。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models fail systematically on long-horizon manipulation tasks despite strong short-horizon performance. We show that this failure is not resolved by extending context length alone in the current reactive execution setting; instead, it stems from three recurring execution-loop deficiencies: the memory gap, the verification gap, and the recovery gap. We present HELM, a model-agnostic framework that addresses these deficiencies with three components: an Episodic Memory Module (EMM) that retrieves key task history via CLIP-indexed keyframes, a learned State Verifier (SV) that predicts action failure before execution from observation, action, subgoal, and memory-conditioned context, and a Harness Controller (HC) that performs rollback and replanning. The SV is the core learning contribution: it consistently outperforms rule-based feasibility checks and ensemble uncertainty baselines, and its effectiveness depends critically on access to episodic memory. On LIBERO-LONG, HELM improves task success rate by 23.1 percentage points over OpenVLA (58.4% to 81.5%), while extending the context window to H=32 yields only a 5.4-point gain and same-budget LoRA adaptation remains 12.2 points below HELM. HELM also improves long-horizon performance on CALVIN and substantially boosts recovery success under controlled perturbations. Ablations and mechanism analyses isolate the contribution of each component, and we release LIBERO-Recovery as a perturbation-injection protocol for evaluating failure recovery in long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [107] [Preserving Clusters in Error-Bounded Lossy Compression of Particle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18801)
*Congrong Ren,Sheng Di,Katrin Heitmann,Franck Cappello,Hanqi Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于校正的技术，在保持压缩性能的同时，确保有损压缩后的粒子数据能够保留单链接聚类结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用中的大规模粒子数据集（如宇宙学、分子动力学、流体动力学）通常使用有损压缩来减少存储和I/O成本，但这些压缩器只提供粒子位置的点状误差边界，无法保证聚类结果的保留。即使微小的扰动也可能改变簇的连通性，从而损害科学有效性。

Method: 提出基于校正的技术，在现成压缩器（如SZ3和Draco）解压数据上操作。包括：(1) 通过空间分区和局部邻域搜索识别脆弱粒子对的聚类感知校正算法；(2) 使用投影梯度下降和编码成对距离违规的损失函数来强制聚类一致性的优化公式；(3) 针对大规模数据集的可扩展GPU加速和分布式实现。

Result: 在宇宙学和分子动力学数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效保留聚类结果，同时在与SZ3、ZFP、Draco、LCP和基于空间填充曲线的方案相比时，保持有竞争力的压缩性能。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了有损压缩中聚类结构保留的关键问题，为科学数据分析提供了可靠的压缩解决方案，确保下游分析的完整性。

Abstract: Lossy compression is widely used to reduce storage and I/O costs for large-scale particle datasets in scientific applications such as cosmology, molecular dynamics, and fluid dynamics, where clustering structures (e.g., single-linkage or Friends-of-Friends) are critical for downstream analysis; however, existing compressors typically provide only pointwise error bounds on particle positions and offer no guarantees on preserving clustering outcomes, and even small perturbations can alter cluster connectivity and compromise scientific validity. We propose a correction-based technique to preserve single-linkage clustering under lossy compression, operating on decompressed data from off-the-shelf compressors such as SZ3 and Draco. Our key contributions are threefold: (1) a clustering-aware correction algorithm that identifies vulnerable particle pairs via spatial partitioning and local neighborhood search; (2) an optimization-based formulation that enforces clustering consistency using projected gradient descent with a loss that encodes pairwise distance violations; and (3) a scalable GPU-accelerated and distributed implementation for large-scale datasets. Experiments on cosmology and molecular dynamics datasets show that our method effectively preserves clustering results while maintaining competitive compression performance compared with SZ3, ZFP, Draco, LCP, and space-filling-curve-based schemes.

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking Dataset Distillation: Hard Truths about Soft Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18811)
*Priyam Dey,Aditya Sahdev,Sunny Bhati,Konda Reddy Mopuri,R. Venkatesh Babu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大规模数据集蒸馏方法在软标签训练下与随机基线表现相当，而在硬标签设置下核心集方法优势明显。论文提出计算感知的CAD-Prune指标和CA2D方法，在ImageNet-1K上优于现有DD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模数据集蒸馏方法在软标签训练下与随机图像基线表现相似，这与核心集文献中高质量核心集在硬标签设置下明显优于随机子集的发现相矛盾。需要理解数据质量在不同标签机制下的作用差异。

Method: 1) 进行可扩展性分析，研究数据质量在软标签+知识蒸馏(SL+KD)、固定软标签(SL)和硬标签(HL)三种标签机制下的作用；2) 系统评估9种DD方法在HL设置下的表现；3) 提出CAD-Prune计算感知剪枝指标，识别给定计算预算下的最优难度样本；4) 开发CA2D计算对齐DD方法。

Result: 1) 高质量核心集在SL和SL+KD机制下无法显著优于随机基线；2) SL+KD设置下性能接近全数据集最优水平，与子集大小或质量无关；3) 在HL设置下，仅RDED在ImageNet-1K上可靠优于随机基线；4) CAD-Prune和CA2D方法在ImageNet-1K各种IPC设置下优于当前DD方法。

Conclusion: 软标签评估实践存在问题，因为子集质量影响可忽略。硬标签设置更能体现数据质量差异。提出的CAD-Prune指标和CA2D方法为数据高效学习提供了有用工具，推动了核心集和数据集蒸馏研究的发展。

Abstract: Despite the perceived success of large-scale dataset distillation (DD) methods, recent evidence finds that simple random image baselines perform on-par with state-of-theart DD methods like SRe2L due to the use of soft labels during downstream model training. This is in contrast with the findings in coreset literature, where high-quality coresets consistently outperform random subsets in the hardlabel (HL) setting. To understand this discrepancy, we perform a detailed scalability analysis to examine the role of data quality under different label regimes, ranging from abundant soft labels (termed as SL+KD regime) to fixed soft labels (SL) and hard labels (HL). Our analysis reveals that high-quality coresets fail to convincingly outperform the random baseline in both SL and SL+KD regimes. In the SL+KD setting, performance further approaches nearoptimal levels relative to the full dataset, regardless of subset size or quality, for a given compute budget. This performance saturation calls into question the widespread practice of using soft labels for model evaluation, where unlike the HL setting, subset quality has negligible influence. A subsequent systematic evaluation of five large-scale and four small-scale DD methods in the HL setting reveals that only RDED reliably outperforms random baselines on ImageNet-1K, but can still lag behind strong coreset methods due to its over-reliance on easy sample patches. Based on this, we introduce CAD-Prune, a compute-aware pruning metric that efficiently identifies samples of optimal difficulty for a given compute budget, and use it to develop CA2D, a compute-aligned DD method, outperforming current DD methods on ImageNet-1K at various IPC settings. Together, our findings uncover many insights into current DD research and establish useful tools to advance dataefficient learning for both coresets and DD.

</details>


### [109] [Curvature-Aware PCA with Geodesic Tangent Space Aggregation for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18816)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GTSA-PCA是一种几何扩展的PCA方法，通过曲率加权的局部协方差算子和测地线对齐，在统一谱框架中结合流形学习的非线性与PCA的谱结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA的全局线性公式无法捕捉弯曲流形上的数据结构，而流形学习方法虽然能建模非线性，但往往牺牲了PCA的谱结构和稳定性。需要一种方法既能处理非线性流形结构，又能保持PCA的谱特性。

Method: 提出测地线切空间聚合PCA（GTSA-PCA）：1）用曲率加权的局部协方差算子替代全局协方差算子，这些算子定义在k近邻图上，产生适应流形并抑制高曲率失真的局部切子空间；2）引入测地线对齐算子，结合内在图距离和子空间亲和度来全局同步这些局部表示；3）该算子允许谱分解，其主导分量定义了几何感知嵌入；4）进一步整合半监督信息来指导对齐，以最小监督改善判别结构。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验显示，GTSA-PCA相比PCA、核PCA、监督PCA以及UMAP等强图基线的持续改进，特别是在小样本量和高曲率情况下表现优异。

Conclusion: GTSA-PCA作为统计方法和几何方法之间的原则性桥梁，为维度降维提供了统一的谱框架，成功整合了曲率感知和测地线一致性。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is a fundamental tool for representation learning, but its global linear formulation fails to capture the structure of data supported on curved manifolds. In contrast, manifold learning methods model nonlinearity but often sacrifice the spectral structure and stability of PCA. We propose \emph{Geodesic Tangent Space Aggregation PCA (GTSA-PCA)}, a geometric extension of PCA that integrates curvature awareness and geodesic consistency within a unified spectral framework. Our approach replaces the global covariance operator with curvature-weighted local covariance operators defined over a $k$-nearest neighbor graph, yielding local tangent subspaces that adapt to the manifold while suppressing high-curvature distortions. We then introduce a geodesic alignment operator that combines intrinsic graph distances with subspace affinities to globally synchronize these local representations. The resulting operator admits a spectral decomposition whose leading components define a geometry-aware embedding. We further incorporate semi-supervised information to guide the alignment, improving discriminative structure with minimal supervision. Experiments on real datasets show consistent improvements over PCA, Kernel PCA, Supervised PCA and strong graph-based baselines such as UMAP, particularly in small sample size and high-curvature regimes. Our results position GTSA-PCA as a principled bridge between statistical and geometric approaches to dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [110] [One Step Forward and K Steps Back: Better Reasoning with Denoising Recursion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18839)
*Chris Cameron,Wangzheng Wang,Nikita Ivanov,Ashmita Bhattacharyya,Didier Chételat,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Denoising Recursion Models方法，通过多步递归去噪训练，解决循环transformer在迭代细化中训练与测试不对齐的问题，在ARC-AGI任务上超越TRM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环transformer通过共享参数块实现计算深度扩展，用于迭代细化任务。但在需要搜索式计算的困难问题中，从噪声到结构化解决方案需要长细化轨迹，而训练时只有目标解监督，缺乏中间路径指导。扩散模型通过单步去噪训练，但训练与测试行为不对齐。

Method: 提出Denoising Recursion Models方法：1) 类似扩散模型对数据添加噪声；2) 但训练模型通过多步递归步骤逆转噪声；3) 提供可处理的中间状态课程；4) 更好对齐训练与测试；5) 激励非贪婪、前瞻性生成。

Result: 通过大量实验，该方法在ARC-AGI任务上超越了最近取得突破性性能的Tiny Recursion Model (TRM)。

Conclusion: Denoising Recursion Models通过多步递归去噪训练，解决了训练与测试不对齐问题，提供了更好的中间状态课程，在需要迭代细化的困难任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Looped transformers scale computational depth without increasing parameter count by repeatedly applying a shared transformer block and can be used for iterative refinement, where each loop rewrites a full fixed-size prediction in parallel. On difficult problems, such as those that require search-like computation, reaching a highly structured solution starting from noise can require long refinement trajectories. Learning such trajectories is challenging when training specifies only the target solution and provides no supervision over the intermediate refinement path. Diffusion models tackle this issue by corrupting data with varying magnitudes of noise and training the model to reverse it in a \textit{single step}. However, this process misaligns training and testing behaviour. We introduce Denoising Recursion Models, a method that similarly corrupts data with noise but trains the model to reverse the corruption over \textit{multiple} recursive steps. This strategy provides a tractable curriculum of intermediate states, while better aligning training with testing and incentivizing non-greedy, forward-looking generation. Through extensive experiments, we show this approach outperforms the Tiny Recursion Model (TRM) on ARC-AGI, where it recently achieved breakthrough performance.

</details>


### [111] [Task Switching Without Forgetting via Proximal Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18857)
*Pourya Shamsolmoali,Masoumeh Zareapoor,Eric Granger,William A. P. Smith,Yue Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于算子分裂的持续学习方法，将任务学习与稳定性执行分离，通过交替优化解决稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，无需回放缓冲区或元学习组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习方法通过正则化项直接添加到训练损失中，将学习和保留信号混合在单一更新中，没有明确分离关键参数与冗余参数。随着任务序列增长，这种耦合会过度约束模型，限制前向传递并导致容量利用效率低下。

Method: 采用算子分裂方法将任务学习与稳定性执行分离：学习步骤专注于最小化当前任务损失，而近端稳定性步骤应用稀疏正则化器来剪枝不必要参数并保留任务相关参数。这使稳定性-可塑性权衡变为两个互补算子之间的协商更新，而非冲突梯度。

Result: 在标准基准测试中实现了最先进的结果，同时提高了稳定性和适应性，无需回放缓冲区、贝叶斯采样或元学习组件。

Conclusion: 通过算子分裂方法将学习与稳定性分离，能够更有效地解决持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，提供了一种理论上有依据且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In continual learning, the primary challenge is to learn new information without forgetting old knowledge. A common solution addresses this trade-off through regularization, penalizing changes to parameters critical for previous tasks. In most cases, this regularization term is directly added to the training loss and optimized with standard gradient descent, which blends learning and retention signals into a single update and does not explicitly separate essential parameters from redundant ones. As task sequences grow, this coupling can over-constrain the model, limiting forward transfer and leading to inefficient use of capacity. We propose a different approach that separates task learning from stability enforcement via operator splitting. The learning step focuses on minimizing the current task loss, while a proximal stability step applies a sparse regularizer to prune unnecessary parameters and preserve task-relevant ones.
